  private updateWeather(): void {
    const w = this.world;
    const weather = w.weather;
    const pty = Math.floor(this.player.cy / TILE);
    const gl = w.groundLevel;
    const rl = Math.max(gl + 1, w.rockLevel);
    // atmo 精确公式（Main.cs:62622：(y/16-(65+10×(maxTilesX/4200)²))/(worldSurface/5)，y=视口中心）；
    // 旧版深度线性近似已废弃
    const cam = this.camera;
    const viewCY = cam ? cam.y : this.player.cy;
    const atmoBase = (viewCY / 16 - (65 + 10 * (w.w / 4200) ** 2)) / Math.max(1, gl / 5);
    const atmo = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, atmoBase));
    // 墓园强度逼近（SceneState.cs:415-418 MoveTowards 0.02/0.1）——target 改吃
    // 可视窗计数(Main 同源 SceneMetrics.Scan VisualScanArea,2026-08-20 对齐;
    // 曾用 Zone 窗 graveyardTileCount,窗口不同计数口径不同)
    const gyTarget = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, (this.biomeCounts.graveyard - 16) / (36 - 16)));
    const gyStep = this.graveyardIntensity < gyTarget ? 0.02 : 0.1;
    this.graveyardIntensity += Math.max(-gyStep, Math.min(gyStep, gyTarget - this.graveyardIntensity));
    // 白天 tick 进度（原版 time 0-54000 ↔ 我们 timeOfDay 白天前半段 0.25-0.75 线性）
    const dayTimeTicks = w.clock.isDay ? ((w.clock.timeOfDay - 0.25) / 0.5) * 54000 : 0;
    // 行为录制：雨切换沿（原版 StartRain/StopRain 无公告——沿检测是唯一锚点）
    if (weather.raining !== this._recLastRaining) {
      this._recLastRaining = weather.raining;
      this.recLog('world', { ev: 'rain', on: weather.raining });
    }
    weather.setWorldSize(w.w, w.h);
    weather.update({
      dayRate: w.dayRate(), // Main.cs:64320-64409 天气全链吃 dayRate（旅程倍率/冻结/全员睡×5/晷快进，曾写死 1）
      dayTime: w.clock.isDay,
      dayTimeTicks,
      playerStrongEnough: this.player.maxHp >= 120,
      playerReadyForKingSlime: this.player.maxHp > 140 && this.player.defense > 8, // Main.cs:64645
      expertMode: w.isExpert,   // Main.expertMode（Main.cs:2775）：史莱姆雨自然触发门（:64373-64409 就绪||专家）
      zoneRain: !!this.scene?.zoneRain,        // ZoneRain 派生位（SceneMetrics.cs:700——暴风雪门 SceneState.cs:102）
      atmo,
      aboveRockLayer: pty < rl,
      graveyardIntensity: this.graveyardIntensity,
      hardMode: !!w.flags.hardMode,
      noOtherEvent: !w.clock.bloodMoon && this.world.invasionType === 0,
      downedSlimeKing: !!(w.flags.downedSlimeKing ?? w.flags.downed_50),  // 击败键=downed_50(50=史莱姆王)
      maxTilesX: w.w,
      worldSurface: gl,
      viewCenterY: viewCY,
      // Main.BackgroundEnabled（Main.cs:790）：设置项「背景 开/关」（Options.backgrounds，
      // 默认 true）——cloudBGAlpha 阴天渐变门（Main.cs:58783）
      backgroundEnabled: options.data.backgrounds,
      // 灯笼夜天气压制（Main.cs:64288 云钳 30 / :64331 停雨 / :64353 不开新雨 /
      // :58249 风场停掷）
      lanternsUp: LanternNight.lanternsUp,
      nextNightIsLanternNight: LanternNight.nextNightIsLanternNight,
      // 旅程力量·冻结风/雨（CreativePowers.cs:1482/:1516——Main.cs:58247/:64329/:64357
      // 原版消费点无 IsJourneyMode 门：旅程角色在经典世界开力量同样生效，状态随
      // 该世界存档；非旅程世界缺省 false 无行为差）
      freezeWind: !!w.journeyPowers?.freezeWind,
      freezeRain: !!w.journeyPowers?.freezeRain,
      // 天气环境音循环门（SceneState.cs:101/190-191）：雪原带 + 是否在背景墙后
      zoneSnow: !!this.scene?.zoneSnow,
      behindBackwall: !!this.scene && (() => {
        // SceneMetrics.cs:671 BehindBackwall = 玩家所在格 wall>0
        const st = w.store;
        const ptx = Math.floor(this.player.cx / TILE);
        return st.inBounds(ptx, pty) && st.wall[st.idx(ptx, pty)] !== 0;
      })(),
      onAmbience: (v) => this.applyWeatherLoops(v),
      // 金币雨玩家锚定（WorldGen.cs:71825 FindClosest 单机等价）
      playerForCoinRain: { x: this.player.cx, active: true, dead: this.player.dead },
      onCoinRain: (x, y, type, stack) => {

        for (let dx = 0; dx <= 1 && !solid; dx++) for (let dy = 0; dy <= 1; dy++) {
          if (st.inBounds(tx0 + dx, ty0 + dy) && st.isSolid(tx0 + dx, ty0 + dy)) { solid = true; break; }

        const key = VI(type);
        this.spawnDrop(x, y, key, stack, 0, 3);
      },

        // 原版 SoundID 43：thunderDistance×15px 处发声——按距离衰减近似

export class Clock {
  /** 昼夜边界（1:1 Main.time：昼 4:30AM-7:30PM=54000t、夜 32400t；0=午夜线性 24h） */
  static readonly DAWN = 4.5 / 24;   // 0.1875
  static readonly DUSK = 19.5 / 24;  // 0.8125
  timeOfDay: number = 8.25 / 24;   // 0-1，0=午夜 0.5=正午（起始 8:15AM = Main.time 13500）
  dayCount: number = 1;
  /** 月相 0-7（0=满月 4=新月；每黎明 +1 mod 8，Main.cs:64877）。对话月相池/血月门用 */
  moonPhase: number = 0;
  /** 血月旗标（dusk roll / dawn 清除，Main.cs:64817-64831） */
  bloodMoon: boolean = false;
  /** 日食旗标（Main.eclipse，随存档持久化——WorldFile.cs:1277-1278 写 / 2070-2072
   *  读，serialize meta.eclipse 同语义）：黎明 roll（Main.cs:64899-64920，
   *  hardMode && downedMechBossAny && 1/20）/ 日耀碑牌召唤（Player.cs:43308，白天），
   *  夜幕清除（Main.cs:64721-64725）——全天持续，只影响白天刷怪段。
   *  触发/清除实现在 world/Eclipse.ts */
  eclipse: boolean = false;
  /** 圣诞季（现实日期 12/15-31，checkXMas Main.cs:13054） */
  xMas: boolean = false;
  /** 万圣季（现实日期 10/20-11/10，checkHalloween Main.cs:13093） */
  halloween: boolean = false;
  readonly dayLengthMs: number;
  constructor(dayLengthMs: number) { this.dayLengthMs = dayLengthMs; }
  tick(dtMs: number) {
    // 原版 Main.time 恒速 tick（1t/帧）：dayLengthMs=全周期 24min 时，0.625/0.375 的
    // 昼夜窗口自然得出 15min/9min——★勿再按段调速（曾除反导致昼夜缩水，探针抓回）
    this.timeOfDay += dtMs / this.dayLengthMs;
    while (this.timeOfDay >= 1) { this.timeOfDay -= 1; this.dayCount++; }
  }
  get isDay(): boolean { return this.timeOfDay > Clock.DAWN && this.timeOfDay < Clock.DUSK; }
  get dayFactor(): number {
    // 昼夜平滑系数：白天 1，夜晚 0.32（月光下地表仍可见，火把不再像贴在黑幕上），晨昏过渡
    const t = this.timeOfDay;
    if (t > Clock.DAWN + 0.04 && t < Clock.DUSK - 0.04) return 1;
    if (t >= Clock.DUSK - 0.04 && t < Clock.DUSK + 0.04) return 1 - (t - (Clock.DUSK - 0.04)) / 0.08 * 0.68;
    if (t >= Clock.DUSK + 0.04 || t < Clock.DAWN - 0.04) return 0.32;
    return 0.32 + (t - (Clock.DAWN - 0.04)) / 0.08 * 0.68;
  }
  get hourFloat(): number { return this.timeOfDay * 24; }
}

Color * num28: XNA Color * float multiplies all 4 channels (r,g,b,a) by num28 clamped 0-255. So vertex color rgb = color.rgb * num28, alpha = 255*num28.

Canvas2D rendering plan:
For each band i: compute 141 samples (k=0..140) of {bottom pt (v), top pt (v2), color rgb, color2 rgb, alpha num28}. Then draw the strip as quads between consecutive samples. Since consecutive samples share edges, drawing quads (k → k+1) each with a linear gradient bottom→top (color*num28 → color2*num28) is the straightforward fill. To avoid per-frame allocation of gradient objects, use the offscreen strip-texture approach:

- offscreen canvas STRIP_W=141, STRIP_H=2. Per band: write ImageData row 0 = bottom colors (color*num28, alpha=255*num28), row 1 = top colors (color2*num28, alpha 255*num28), putImageData once. Then for each segment k in 0..139: drawImage(offscreen, k, 0, 1, 2, ...) mapped to the quad via setTransform parallelogram: origin at bottom point of sample k+1 (or k), x-axis along strip (from sample k to k+1 bottom edge), y-axis from bottom to top.

Parallelogram mapping: transform(a,b,c,d,e,f) maps unit square (u,v) → (a*u + c*v + e, b*u + d*v + f). Take u along bottom edge from A_k to A_{k+1}, v from bottom to top (A_k to B_k):
a = A_{k+1}.x - A_k.x, b = A_{k+1}.y - A_k.y, c = B_k.x - A_k.x, d = B_k.y - A_k.y, e = A_k.x, f = A_k.y.
drawImage(off, k, 0, 1, 2, 0, 0, 1, 1) with that transform → maps source column k (width 1px from x=k) stretched over u∈[0,1], and rows 0..2 over v∈[0,1]. That gives bilinear-interpolated colors ≈ Gouraud vertex colors.

Linear filter samples the two nearest texels: for u_dest ∈ [0,1], src coordinate ∈ [k, k+1] — at edges u=0 samples texel k exactly (clamp/linear at texel center?). private rasterBand(ctx, bandIdx, scale) {
  // 填 strip 纹理(141×2):row0=底边色,row1=顶边色(白)

    d[q] = d[q+1] = d[q+2] = 255*a... 
  }

    this._opacity -= dtSec * 0.5;   // :41
    if (this._opacity < 0) { this._isActive = false; this._opacity = 0; }  // :42-46
  } else {
    this._opacity += dtSec * 0.3;   // :50
    if (this._opacity > 1) this._opacity = 1;   // :51-54
  }
}

      // Color*float 负值钳 0\n    }\n  }\n\n  /** 单带光栅:141×2 条带纹理(底边色→白)→140 段仿射切片(顶点色 Gouraud 等价)。\n   *  像素=(color.RGB·num28, α=num28) 非预乘入 ImageData;source-over 合成\n   *  = color·num28²+dst·(1−num28) —— XNA AlphaBlend(地表背景批次态,Main.cs:56294)\n   *  ×顶点色(Shader 输出 RGB 已乘顶点色)的逐字结果【shader 推断:shader 输出\n   *  是否再乘顶点色无源码可证,取标准乘法语义】。 */\n  private rasterBand(ctx: CanvasRenderingContext2D, band: number, bands: number): void {\n    if (!this.stripData) {\n      if (typeof document === 'undefined') return;\n      this.stripCanvas = document.createElement('canvas');\n      this.stripCanvas.width = AURORA_SEG + 1;\n      this.stripCanvas.height = 2;\n      this.stripCtx = this.stripCanvas.getContext('2d');\n      if (!this.stripCtx) return;\n      this.stripData = this.stripCtx.createImageData(AURORA_SEG + 1, 2);\n    }\n    const sd = this.stripData;\n    const d = sd.data;\n    const base = band * (AURORA_SEG + 1);\n    for (let k = 0; k <= AURORA_SEG; k++) {\n      const o = base + k;\n      const a = this.sA[o];\n      const ab = Math.round(a * 255);                     // α=num28\n      let p = k * 8;                                      // row0=下缘 color·num28\n      d[p] = Math.max(0, Math.min(255, Math.round(this.sR[o] * a)));\n      d[p + 1] = Math.max(0, Math.min(255, Math.round(this.sG[o] * a)));\n      d[p + 2] = Math.max(0, Math.min(255, Math.round(this.sB[o] * a)));\n      d[p + 3] = ab;\n      p = ((AURORA_SEG + 1) + k) * 8;                     // row1=上缘 color2(白)·num28\n      const w = ab;\n      d[p] = w; d[p + 1] = w; d[p + 2] = w; d[p + 3] = ab;\n    }\n    this.stripCtx!.putImageData(sd, 0, 0);\n    const strip = this.stripCanvas!;\n    ctx.save();\n    ctx.imageSmoothingEnabled = true;\n    for (let k = AURORA_SEG; k >= 1; k--) {\n      // 段 k:采样 k(num10=k/140)→k−1;源列 (k−1..k) 跨段线性 = 顶点色插值\n      const a = base + k, b = base + k - 1;\n      const ax = this.sBotX[a], ay = this.sBotY[a];\n      const uX = this.sBotX[b] - ax, uY = this.sBotY[b] - ay;  "new_string": "// 闪电白帧状态(Main.cs:63346-63355:bgColor 三通道向 1 lerp lightning——\n// 闪白走 ColorOfTheSkies 链:天空贴图/远景/光种子同步闪亮;由 Renderer 写入。\n// 位置在日食后、地平线渗入前,与原版序一致)\nlet frameLightning = 0;\nexport function setFrameLightning(v: number): void {\n  frameLightning = v;\n}\n// ---- 极光渗入帧状态(AuroraSky.ModifyTileColor,SetBackColor 尾 Main.cs:63360;\n// 调用序:HorizonRenderer.ModifyHorizonLight(:63356)→ ModifyTileColor(:63360)→\n// ColorOfTheSkies 赋值(:63361),即晨昏地平线渗入【之后】。intensity=0.08 调用点\n// 参数;门 = AuroraSky._opacity>0(:412)且月相≠QuarterAtLeft(:414-416);\n// lerp 目标 = _lastSkyColor 强制 A=255(:417-418),amount = opacity×0.08(:419)) ----\nexport interface AuroraFrameState {\n  /** _lastSkyColor(0-255,DrawAuroraSky :376-379 写入) */\n  rgb: readonly [number, number, number];\n  /** _opacity */\n  opacity: number;\n  /** 实时月相(ModifyTileColor 读 Main.GetMoonPhase()) */\n  phase: number;\n}\nlet frameAurora: AuroraFrameState | null = null;\nexport function setFrameAurora(v: AuroraFrameState | null): void {\n  frameAurora = v;\n}\n/** 极光渗入(Color.Lerp 逐通道,:419)——供 skySeed/colorOfTheSkies 尾段复用 */\nfunction applyAuroraTileColor(bg: [number, number, number], a: AuroraFrameState): void {\n  if (a.phase === 3 /* MoonPhase.QuarterAtLeft :415 */) return;\n  const amt = a.opacity * 0.08;                       // :419 opacity×intensity(0.08)\n  bg[0] += (a.rgb[0] - bg[0]) * amt;\n  bg[1] += (a.rgb[1] - bg[1]) * amt;\n  bg[2] += (a.rgb[2] - bg[2]) * amt;\n}"
}

For test 5, MOON_FLOOR[0]=19 verified from existing test. Deep night at timeOfDay=0: bg=[19,19,19]. aurora rgb [200,50,50] opacity 0.5 phase 0 → k=0.04 → [26.24, 20.24, 20.24].

Note: frame state pollution across tests in the same file — each test sets its own state via setFrameAurora(null) at start.

Also test the re-activation quirk: setActive(false) → leaving; update(0.5) → opacity 0.5; setActive(true) → still leaving (no reactivate since _isActive still true); update(1) → 0, inactive. Then setActive(true) → active again.
```

</details>

 "old_string": "  /** 单带光栅:141×2 条带纹理(底边色→白)→140 段仿射切片(顶点色 Gouraud 等价)。",
 "new_string": "  /** Main.hslToRgb 直写采样数组(hslToRgb255 同式,零分配路径——段循环 423 次/帧) */\n  private hslInto(hue: number, saturation: number, luminosity: number, o: number): void {\n    const b = (c: number, t1: number, t2: number) => {\n      if (c < 0) c += 1;\n      if (c > 1) c -= 1;\n      if (6 * c < 1) return t1 + (t2 - t1) * 6 * c;\n      if (2 * c < 1) return t2;\n      if (3 * c < 2) return t1 + (t2 - t1) * (2 / 3 - c) * 6;\n      return t1;\n    };\n    if (saturation === 0) {\n      const v = Math.round(luminosity * 255);\n      this.sR[o] = v; this.sG[o] = v; this.sB[o] = v;\n      return;\n    }\n    const q = luminosity < 0.5 ? luminosity * (1 + saturation) : luminosity + saturation - luminosity * saturation;\n    const t = 2 * luminosity - q;\n    this.sR[o] = Math.round(b(hue + 1 / 3, t, q) * 255);\n    this.sG[o] = Math.round(b(hue, t, q) * 255);\n    this.sB[o] = Math.round(b(hue - 1 / 3, t, q) * 255);\n  }\n\n  /** 单带光栅:141×2 条带纹理(底边色→白)→140 段仿射切片(顶点色 Gouraud 等价)。"
}

To draw the segment in base-transformed space, I want: final = baseM ∘ segM. ctx.setTransform(segM) then ctx.transform(baseM) gives final = baseM × segM — which maps user space through segM then baseM — that IS base ∘ seg. Yes correct: transform() post-multiplies the current matrix: current becomes current × arg... Actually CanvasRenderingContext2D.transform(a,b,c,d,e,f) = "multiplies the current transformation with the argument" — the new CTM = current × arg (arg applied first in user space, then current). So setTransform(seg) then transform(base) → CTM = seg × base — meaning user coords go through base FIRST, then seg. That's wrong order — I want user point → seg (unit quad → screen-ish) → base (DPR scale). CTM should be base × seg, i.e. user → seg → base. With setTransform(segM) then transform(baseM): per spec, transform(m) results in CTM_new = CTM_old × m. So CTM = segM × baseM → user → baseM → segM. Wrong.

Correct: setTransform(baseM) then transform(seg) → CTM = baseM × segM → user → seg → base ✓. Let me fix: setTransform(baseM.a... baseM.f) then ctx.transform(uX, uY, vX, vY, ax, ay), draw, and no per-iteration restore needed... still need to reset for next iteration: setTransform(baseM) at start of each iteration then transform(seg). ```

  ctx.transform(uX, uY, vX, vY, ax, ay);

}
```

  private hslInto(hue: number, saturation: number, luminosity: number, o: number): void {
    const b = (c: number, t1: number, t2: number) => {
      if (c < 0) c += 1;
      if (c > 1) c -= 1;
      if (6 * c < 1) return t1 + (t2 - t1) * 6 * c;
      if (2 * c < 1) return t2;
      if (3 * c < 2) return t1 + (t2 - t1) * (2 / 3 - c) * 6;
      return t1;
    };

  /** 极光绘制(AuroraSky.cs Draw/DrawAuroraSky):帧参数组装 + 地表门输入。
   *  月锚 = Main.LastCelestialBodyPosition(Main.cs:62458-62459 月屏位/ScreenSize;
   *  AuroraSky.cs:129-130 Y 侧再乘 (1920/1080)/(屏宽/屏高) 纵横比较正)——
   *  moonScreen 由本帧月亮分支写入(满月分支带 1/2 磁吸月位 :356-358 消费)。 */
  private drawAurora(ctx: CanvasRenderingContext2D, viewW: number, viewH: number,
    camY: number, dtMs: number, nightTicks: number, isDay: boolean, moonPhase: number): void {
    const zQ = this.frameCtx?.z ?? 1;
    const camTopTiles = (camY - viewH / 2 / zQ) / 16;   // Main.screenPosition.Y/16
    const ms = this.moonScreen;
    const moonFrac = ms
      ? { x: ms.x / viewW, y: (ms.y / viewH) * (1920 / 1080) / (viewW / viewH) }
      : null;
    this.aurora.draw(ctx, viewW, viewH, {
      nightTicks, isDay, moonPhase,
      camTopTiles,
      surfaceTiles: this.fx.surfaceY / 16,
      moonFrac,
      dtSec: dtMs / 1000,
    });
  }

  /** 派对气球绘制（PartySky.Update/Draw :130-198 精简核：上升 + 风漂移 + 视差 1/depth） */

## 报告

### 改动文件
| 文件 | 改动 |
|---|---|
| `game/src/render/AuroraSky.ts` | **新建**,AuroraSky.cs 全文 1:1 移植(纯函数 + 状态机 + Canvas2D 条带渲染,零依赖零每帧分配) |
| `game/src/render/SkyRenderer.ts` | `readonly aurora` 实例;`drawAurora()` 组帧(地表门/月锚换算);`draw()` 在远距云之后插入极光;`clearAmbience()` 加 `aurora.reset()` |
| `game/src/render/Renderer.ts` | `setFrameAurora` 帧状态写入(与既有 setFrame* 同点,`sky.draw` 前,同 horizonVis 一帧滞后模式) |
| `game/src/lighting/SkyColor.ts` | `AuroraFrameState`/`setFrameAurora` + `applyAuroraTileColor` lerp;接入 `skySeed` 与 `colorOfTheSkies`(均在 ModifyHorizonLight 渗入之后) |
| `game/src/core/Game.ts` | `updateWeather()` 内激活门:`zoneSnow && !isDay && !(zoneRain&&zoneSnow) && graveyardIntensity<0.5`(每 tick,不动 15tick 扫描结构) |
| `game/tests/aurora-sky.test.ts` | **新建** 14 用例(月相门/lastSkyColor 锚值/180tick 门/淡入出速率/重入怪癖/lerp 数值) |

### 关键决策
- **绘制层序(与任务描述不同,以反编译为准)**:核实 AuroraSky.Draw 只在 `maxDepth==MaxValue` 层画 = DrawSurfaceBG 内首个 `DrawToDepth(1/0.09)`(Main.cs:58829),即**远距云(:58757)之后、云背景层之前;日月(:56310 DrawSunAndMoon)画在极光之前**(任务背景里"日月之前"经核实为误记,已按源码序接线并注释)。
- **激活/淡出**:`setActive` 内置 ManageSpecialBiomeVisuals(SceneState.cs:123-133)的 `inZone != IsActive()` 守卫——含"淡出中重入不重 Activate"的原版怪癖(有锁测试)。淡入 +0.3/s、淡出 −0.5/s 按 gameTime 秒计;SkyManager.Update 的 dayRate 倍速加速未镜像(登记)。
- **几何**:141 段/带,1920×1080 参考空间 × `scale=屏宽/1920`;死代码(算后被覆写的反编译中间式)不落地、行注释标注;`num7/num8/num19/luminosity` 原版在段内赋值但为带内常量,合成终值(luminosity 全分支恒 1 → 上缘顶点色恒白)。
- **渲染载体**:141×2 复用条带纹理(row0=底边色、row1=白)putImageData 一次 + 140 段仿射 `drawImage` 切片 = 顶点色 Gouraud 插值等价;采样/颜色全部 TypedArray 预分配。
- **月锚**:满月分支带 1/2 磁吸月位(:356-358)与月距淡出(:383-384),月位 = 本帧 `moonScreen`/ScreenSize(:62458-62459)+ Y 侧纵横比较正(:130),静态位持久语义。

### shader 推断部分(诚实清单)
1. 像素着色器本体(DyeInitializer.cs:485-487,噪声贴图 Extra_286/287)编译产物无源码——specificData X/Y/Z/W(:139-189)、噪声采样、uTime 调制**不可复刻**,仅以 CPU 侧顶点色(color/color2×num28,:401)重建。
2. 混合态:DrawTrail 继承地表背景批次 `BlendState.AlphaBlend`(Main.cs:56294)→ Canvas source-over;像素存 `(color·num28, α=num28)` = XNA AlphaBlend×顶点色的字面结果(shader 输出是否再乘顶点色无源码可证,取标准乘法语义)。
3. 背景变换矩阵(:128)按无相机平移的恒等屏空间处理(bg zoom≈1)。
4. `BackgroundEnabled` 选项门未接(极光随所有首候选层被地表门 camTop≤worldSurface+10 统一覆盖)。

### _lastSkyColor 赋值结论
**AuroraSky.cs:376-379:仅带 i==0、段 num9==19(num10=19/140,带 0 无 flag5 重映射)时写入该段 color** —— `hslToRgb((num19 + cos(num10·2π·num7)·0.1)%1, saturation, 0.5)`(:374);带 0 处 num19=num5=GlobalTimeWrappedHourly/60,num7/saturation 按月相(2.5/1、1/1、0.5/1、0.45 档等)。消费:ModifyTileColor(:410-422)强制 A=255 后 `Lerp(tileColor, lastSkyColor, opacity×0.08)`,月相≠QuarterAtLeft 门在消费侧实时读相位。
